// silence unused\n        return supplyShares[lender].toAssetsDown(supplyAssets, totalSupplyShares);\n    }","newString":"    /// @notice Value of a lender's supply shares in loan tokens, interest included.\n    function supplyAssetsOf(address lender) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return supplyShares[lender].toAssetsDown(totalSupplyAssets + _pendingInterest(), totalSupplyShares);\n    }","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport {Ownable2Step} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol\";\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {IERC20Metadata} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/IERC20Metadata.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport {Math} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol\";\nimport {Pausable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Pausable.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\n\nimport {IPriceOracle} from \"./interfaces/IPriceOracle.sol\";\nimport {SharesMathLib} from \"./libraries/SharesMathLib.sol\";\n\n/// @title BorrowMarket\n/// @notice Single-pair overcollateralised borrowing market: lock WETH, borrow USDC.\n///\n/// Lenders supply the loan token and receive shares in the pool; borrowers post collateral and draw\n/// loan tokens against it up to `ltvBps` of the collateral's value. Debt accrues at a flat annual rate.\n/// Once debt exceeds `liquidationThresholdBps` of collateral value, anyone may repay part of the debt\n/// and seize the matching collateral plus `liquidationBonusBps`.\n///\n/// @dev Accounting is share-based on both sides. Interest is materialised into `totalBorrowAssets` /\n///      `totalSupplyAssets` by `accrueInterest()`, which every state-changing entrypoint calls first.\n///      Balances are tracked internally and never read from `balanceOf`, so token donations to this\n///      contract cannot move the exchange rate.\ncontract BorrowMarket is Ownable2Step, Pausable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n    using SharesMathLib for uint256;\n\n    uint256 internal constant WAD = 1e18;\n    uint256 internal constant BPS = 1e4;\n    uint256 internal constant SECONDS_PER_YEAR = 365 days;\n\n    /// @notice Hard ceiling on the configurable borrow rate: 100% APR.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_RATE_PER_YEAR = 1e18;\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                 Immutables                                 */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Collateral asset (WETH on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable collateralToken;\n    /// @notice Borrowable asset (USDC on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable loanToken;\n\n    /// @dev 10 ** decimals of each token, captured once at deploy.\n    uint256 internal immutable collateralScale;\n    uint256 internal immutable loanScale;\n\n    /// @notice Max debt as a fraction of collateral value when *taking on* risk (borrow / withdraw collateral).\n    uint256 public immutable ltvBps;\n    /// @notice Debt/collateral ratio at which a position becomes liquidatable.\n    uint256 public immutable liquidationThresholdBps;\n    /// @notice Extra collateral a liquidator receives, on top of the value repaid.\n    uint256 public immutable liquidationBonusBps;\n    /// @notice Max fraction of a position's debt that a single liquidation may repay while the position\n    ///         is still solvent. Once collateral value no longer covers the debt, full repayment is allowed\n    ///         so the bad debt can be cleared.\n    uint256 public immutable closeFactorBps;\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                   Storage                                  */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    struct Position {\n        uint256 collateral;\n        uint256 borrowShares;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Price source for the collateral asset.\n    IPriceOracle public oracle;\n    /// @notice Flat annual interest rate, 1e18 = 100% APR.\n    uint256 public ratePerYear;\n\n    uint256 public totalSupplyAssets;\n    uint256 public totalSupplyShares;\n    uint256 public totalBorrowAssets;\n    uint256 public totalBorrowShares;\n    uint256 public lastAccrual;\n\n    mapping(address => uint256) public supplyShares;\n    mapping(address => Position) public positions;\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                   Events                                   */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    event Supply(address indexed caller, address indexed onBehalf, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event Withdraw(address indexed caller, address indexed receiver, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event DepositCollateral(address indexed caller, address indexed onBehalf, uint256 amount);\n    event WithdrawCollateral(address indexed caller, address indexed receiver, uint256 amount);\n    event Borrow(address indexed borrower, address indexed receiver, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event Repay(address indexed caller, address indexed onBehalf, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event Liquidate(\n        address indexed liquidator, address indexed borrower, uint256 repaidAssets, uint256 repaidShares, uint256 seized\n    );\n    event BadDebtRealised(address indexed borrower, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event AccrueInterest(uint256 interest, uint256 elapsed);\n    event OracleSet(address indexed oracle);\n    event RateSet(uint256 ratePerYear);\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                   Errors                                   */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error InvalidParameters();\n    error UnsupportedDecimals();\n    error RateTooHigh();\n    error InsufficientLiquidity();\n    error InsufficientShares();\n    error InsufficientCollateral();\n    error PositionUnhealthy();\n    error PositionHealthy();\n    error NoDebt();\n    error RepayExceedsCloseFactor();\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                 Constructor                                */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @param owner_ Admin address. Should be a timelocked multisig — see NOTES.md.\n    /// @param collateralToken_ WETH.\n    /// @param loanToken_ USDC.\n    /// @param oracle_ Collateral price source, 1e18-scaled USD per whole collateral unit.\n    /// @param ratePerYear_ Flat annual borrow rate, 1e18 = 100%.\n    /// @param ltvBps_ Borrow limit, e.g. 7000.\n    /// @param liquidationThresholdBps_ Liquidation trigger, e.g. 8500.\n    /// @param liquidationBonusBps_ Liquidator incentive, e.g. 500.\n    /// @param closeFactorBps_ Max share of debt repayable per liquidation while solvent, e.g. 5000.\n    constructor(\n        address owner_,\n        address collateralToken_,\n        address loanToken_,\n        address oracle_,\n        uint256 ratePerYear_,\n        uint256 ltvBps_,\n        uint256 liquidationThresholdBps_,\n        uint256 liquidationBonusBps_,\n        uint256 closeFactorBps_\n    ) Ownable(owner_) {\n        if (collateralToken_ == address(0) || loanToken_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (collateralToken_ == loanToken_) revert InvalidParameters();\n\n        // Ordering invariants. The last one is the important one: at the moment a position crosses the\n        // liquidation threshold, seizing collateral worth (1 + bonus) x the repaid debt must still be\n        // covered by the collateral on hand, otherwise every liquidation would immediately create bad debt.\n        if (ltvBps_ == 0 || ltvBps_ >= liquidationThresholdBps_) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (liquidationThresholdBps_ >= BPS) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (liquidationBonusBps_ == 0) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (closeFactorBps_ == 0 || closeFactorBps_ > BPS) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (liquidationThresholdBps_ * (BPS + liquidationBonusBps_) > BPS * BPS) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (ratePerYear_ > MAX_RATE_PER_YEAR) revert RateTooHigh();\n\n        collateralToken = IERC20(collateralToken_);\n        loanToken = IERC20(loanToken_);\n\n        uint8 collateralDecimals = IERC20Metadata(collateralToken_).decimals();\n        uint8 loanDecimals = IERC20Metadata(loanToken_).decimals();\n        if (collateralDecimals > 18 || loanDecimals > 18) revert UnsupportedDecimals();\n        collateralScale = 10 ** collateralDecimals;\n        loanScale = 10 ** loanDecimals;\n\n        ltvBps = ltvBps_;\n        liquidationThresholdBps = liquidationThresholdBps_;\n        liquidationBonusBps = liquidationBonusBps_;\n        closeFactorBps = closeFactorBps_;\n\n        _setOracle(oracle_);\n        ratePerYear = ratePerYear_;\n        emit RateSet(ratePerYear_);\n\n        lastAccrual = block.timestamp;\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                  Interest                                  */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Materialise interest owed since the last touch. Safe to call by anyone at any time.\n    /// @dev Simple interest over the elapsed interval, which compounds each time the market is touched.\n    function accrueInterest() public {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrual;\n        if (elapsed == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 borrowed = totalBorrowAssets;\n        if (borrowed != 0 && ratePerYear != 0) {\n            uint256 interest = Math.mulDiv(borrowed, ratePerYear * elapsed, WAD * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n            if (interest != 0) {\n                totalBorrowAssets = borrowed + interest;\n                totalSupplyAssets += interest;\n                emit AccrueInterest(interest, elapsed);\n            }\n        }\n\n        lastAccrual = block.timestamp;\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                 Lender side                                */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Supply loan tokens to the pool and receive shares.\n    function supply(uint256 assets, address onBehalf) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused returns (uint256 shares) {\n        if (assets == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        shares = assets.toSharesDown(totalSupplyAssets, totalSupplyShares);\n        if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        supplyShares[onBehalf] += shares;\n        totalSupplyShares += shares;\n        totalSupplyAssets += assets;\n\n        emit Supply(msg.sender, onBehalf, assets, shares);\n        loanToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), assets);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Burn supply shares and withdraw the underlying loan tokens.\n    /// @dev Limited by idle liquidity: assets that are currently lent out cannot be withdrawn.\n    function withdraw(uint256 shares, address receiver) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 assets) {\n        if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (receiver == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        if (supplyShares[msg.sender] < shares) revert InsufficientShares();\n\n        assets = shares.toAssetsDown(totalSupplyAssets, totalSupplyShares);\n\n        supplyShares[msg.sender] -= shares;\n        totalSupplyShares -= shares;\n        totalSupplyAssets -= assets;\n\n        if (totalBorrowAssets > totalSupplyAssets) revert InsufficientLiquidity();\n\n        emit Withdraw(msg.sender, receiver, assets, shares);\n        loanToken.safeTransfer(receiver, assets);\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                Borrower side                               */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Add collateral to a position.\n    /// @dev Anyone may top up anyone's position; this can only improve the target's health.\n    function depositCollateral(uint256 amount, address onBehalf) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        positions[onBehalf].collateral += amount;\n\n        emit DepositCollateral(msg.sender, onBehalf, amount);\n        collateralToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Remove collateral from your own position. Must leave the position within the LTV limit.\n    function withdrawCollateral(uint256 amount, address receiver) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (receiver == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        Position storage position = positions[msg.sender];\n        if (position.collateral < amount) revert InsufficientCollateral();\n        position.collateral -= amount;\n\n        _requireWithinLtv(position);\n\n        emit WithdrawCollateral(msg.sender, receiver, amount);\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(receiver, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Borrow loan tokens against your own collateral.\n    function borrow(uint256 assets, address receiver) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused returns (uint256 shares) {\n        if (assets == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (receiver == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        shares = assets.toSharesUp(totalBorrowAssets, totalBorrowShares);\n\n        Position storage position = positions[msg.sender];\n        position.borrowShares += shares;\n        totalBorrowShares += shares;\n        totalBorrowAssets += assets;\n\n        if (totalBorrowAssets > totalSupplyAssets) revert InsufficientLiquidity();\n        _requireWithinLtv(position);\n\n        emit Borrow(msg.sender, receiver, assets, shares);\n        loanToken.safeTransfer(receiver, assets);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Repay debt on a position. Pass `type(uint256).max` to repay the position in full.\n    /// @dev Repaying is never paused and never health-checked — it can only make a position safer.\n    function repay(uint256 assets, address onBehalf) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 shares) {\n        if (onBehalf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        Position storage position = positions[onBehalf];\n        uint256 borrowShares_ = position.borrowShares;\n        if (borrowShares_ == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        if (assets == type(uint256).max) {\n            // Full exit: price the caller's whole share balance, rounding the amount owed up.\n            shares = borrowShares_;\n            assets = shares.toAssetsUp(totalBorrowAssets, totalBorrowShares);\n        } else {\n            if (assets == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n            shares = assets.toSharesDown(totalBorrowAssets, totalBorrowShares);\n            if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n            if (shares > borrowShares_) revert InsufficientShares();\n        }\n\n        position.borrowShares = borrowShares_ - shares;\n        totalBorrowShares -= shares;\n        totalBorrowAssets = totalBorrowAssets.zeroFloorSub(assets);\n\n        emit Repay(msg.sender, onBehalf, assets, shares);\n        loanToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), assets);\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                 Liquidation                                */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Repay part of an unhealthy position's debt and seize the matching collateral plus bonus.\n    /// @param borrower Position to liquidate.\n    /// @param repayAssets Loan-token amount to repay. Pass `type(uint256).max` to repay the maximum allowed.\n    /// @return repaid Loan tokens actually pulled from the liquidator.\n    /// @return seized Collateral tokens sent to the liquidator.\n    /// @dev `repaid` may come out below `repayAssets` when the position has less collateral than the\n    ///      requested repayment would seize; the seizure is clamped to the collateral on hand and the\n    ///      repayment recomputed from it. Liquidators should simulate first and set slippage bounds.\n    function liquidate(address borrower, uint256 repayAssets)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        whenNotPaused\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        Position storage position = positions[borrower];\n        uint256 borrowShares_ = position.borrowShares;\n        uint256 collateral = position.collateral;\n        if (borrowShares_ == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        uint256 price = oracle.price();\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueInLoanUnits(collateral, price);\n        uint256 debt = borrowShares_.toAssetsUp(totalBorrowAssets, totalBorrowShares);\n\n        // Healthy positions are off limits.\n        if (debt <= Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, liquidationThresholdBps, BPS)) revert PositionHealthy();\n\n        // While the position is still solvent the close factor bounds a single liquidation. Once the\n        // collateral no longer covers the debt, allow a full repayment so the position can be closed out.\n        uint256 maxRepay =\n            collateralValue <= debt ? debt : Math.mulDiv(debt, closeFactorBps, BPS, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        repaid = repayAssets == type(uint256).max ? maxRepay : repayAssets;\n        if (repaid == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (repaid > maxRepay) revert RepayExceedsCloseFactor();\n\n        seized = _seizeForRepay(repaid, price);\n        if (seized > collateral) {\n            seized = collateral;\n            repaid = _repayForSeize(seized, price);\n        }\n        if (seized == 0 || repaid == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        uint256 repaidShares = repaid.toSharesDown(totalBorrowAssets, totalBorrowShares);\n        if (repaidShares > borrowShares_) repaidShares = borrowShares_;\n\n        borrowShares_ -= repaidShares;\n        position.borrowShares = borrowShares_;\n        position.collateral = collateral - seized;\n        totalBorrowShares -= repaidShares;\n        totalBorrowAssets = totalBorrowAssets.zeroFloorSub(repaid);\n\n        emit Liquidate(msg.sender, borrower, repaid, repaidShares, seized);\n\n        // Collateral exhausted but debt remains: it is unrecoverable. Write it off now so that\n        // totalSupplyAssets stops counting money that will never come back, and lenders share the loss\n        // pro rata at today's exchange rate instead of the last one out taking it all.\n        if (position.collateral == 0 && borrowShares_ != 0) {\n            uint256 badDebt = borrowShares_.toAssetsUp(totalBorrowAssets, totalBorrowShares);\n\n            position.borrowShares = 0;\n            totalBorrowShares -= borrowShares_;\n            totalBorrowAssets = totalBorrowAssets.zeroFloorSub(badDebt);\n            totalSupplyAssets = totalSupplyAssets.zeroFloorSub(badDebt);\n\n            emit BadDebtRealised(borrower, badDebt, borrowShares_);\n        }\n\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(msg.sender, seized);\n        loanToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), repaid);\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                    Views                                   */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Debt of a position including interest accrued up to now, rounded up.\n    function debtOf(address borrower) public view returns (uint256) {\n        (uint256 borrowAssets, uint256 borrowShares_) = _projectedBorrowTotals();\n        return positions[borrower].borrowShares.toAssetsUp(borrowAssets, borrowShares_);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Collateral of a position valued in loan-token units at the current oracle price.\n    function collateralValueOf(address borrower) public view returns (uint256) {\n        return _collateralValueInLoanUnits(positions[borrower].collateral, oracle.price());\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Loan tokens a position could still borrow before hitting the LTV limit.\n    function maxBorrow(address borrower) external view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 limit = Math.mulDiv(collateralValueOf(borrower), ltvBps, BPS);\n        uint256 debt = debtOf(borrower);\n        uint256 headroom = limit.zeroFloorSub(debt);\n        uint256 liquidity = totalSupplyAssets.zeroFloorSub(totalBorrowAssets);\n        return Math.min(headroom, liquidity);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice False once debt exceeds `liquidationThresholdBps` of collateral value, i.e. liquidatable.\n    function isHealthy(address borrower) external view returns (bool) {\n        return debtOf(borrower) <= Math.mulDiv(collateralValueOf(borrower), liquidationThresholdBps, BPS);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Collateral value over debt, 1e18-scaled; below 1e18 the position is liquidatable.\n    /// @dev `type(uint256).max` for a position with no debt.\n    function healthFactor(address borrower) external view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 debt = debtOf(borrower);\n        if (debt == 0) return type(uint256).max;\n        uint256 limit = Math.mulDiv(collateralValueOf(borrower), liquidationThresholdBps, BPS);\n        return Math.mulDiv(limit, WAD, debt);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Loan tokens available to borrow or withdraw right now.\n    function availableLiquidity() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return totalSupplyAssets.zeroFloorSub(totalBorrowAssets);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Value of `shares` supply shares in loan tokens, interest included.\n    function supplyAssetsOf(address lender) external view returns (uint256) {\n        (uint256 supplyAssets, uint256 borrowAssets) = _projectedSupplyTotals();\n        borrowAssets; // silence unused\n        return supplyShares[lender].toAssetsDown(supplyAssets, totalSupplyShares);\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                    Admin                                   */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Point the market at a new price source.\n    /// @dev Highest-impact admin power in the system: a bad oracle can make every position liquidatable.\n    ///      Owner must be a timelock. See NOTES.md.\n    function setOracle(address oracle_) external onlyOwner {\n        _setOracle(oracle_);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Change the flat borrow rate. Interest up to this point is settled at the old rate first.\n    function setRatePerYear(uint256 ratePerYear_) external onlyOwner {\n        if (ratePerYear_ > MAX_RATE_PER_YEAR) revert RateTooHigh();\n        accrueInterest();\n        ratePerYear = ratePerYear_;\n        emit RateSet(ratePerYear_);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Halt supply, borrow, collateral moves and liquidations. Repayment stays open.\n    function pause() external onlyOwner {\n        accrueInterest();\n        _pause();\n    }\n\n    function unpause() external onlyOwner {\n        // Bring `lastAccrual` to now before reopening so the paused interval is not charged as interest\n        // at whatever the rate happens to be on unpause.\n        accrueInterest();\n        _unpause();\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                  Internals                                 */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    function _setOracle(address oracle_) private {\n        if (oracle_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        // Fail fast on a feed that is already broken rather than discovering it on the next borrow.\n        if (IPriceOracle(oracle_).price() == 0) revert InvalidParameters();\n        oracle = IPriceOracle(oracle_);\n        emit OracleSet(oracle_);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Reverts unless the position's debt is within `ltvBps` of its collateral value.\n    ///      Callers must have accrued interest first.\n    function _requireWithinLtv(Position storage position) private view {\n        uint256 debt = position.borrowShares.toAssetsUp(totalBorrowAssets, totalBorrowShares);\n        if (debt == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueInLoanUnits(position.collateral, oracle.price());\n        if (debt > Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, ltvBps, BPS)) revert PositionUnhealthy();\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Collateral amount -> loan-token units at `price` (1e18 USD per whole collateral unit).\n    ///      Rounds down, so the protocol never overstates what a position is worth.\n    function _collateralValueInLoanUnits(uint256 collateral, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 valueUsd = Math.mulDiv(collateral, price, collateralScale);\n        return Math.mulDiv(valueUsd, loanScale, WAD);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Collateral owed to a liquidator for repaying `repayAssets`, including the bonus. Rounds down.\n    function _seizeForRepay(uint256 repayAssets, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 valueUsd = Math.mulDiv(repayAssets, WAD, loanScale);\n        uint256 withBonus = Math.mulDiv(valueUsd, BPS + liquidationBonusBps, BPS);\n        return Math.mulDiv(withBonus, collateralScale, price);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Inverse of `_seizeForRepay`, used when the seizure is clamped to the collateral on hand.\n    ///      Rounds down so the clamped seizure is never under-collateralised relative to the repayment.\n    function _repayForSeize(uint256 seizeAmount, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 withBonusUsd = Math.mulDiv(seizeAmount, price, collateralScale);\n        uint256 valueUsd = Math.mulDiv(withBonusUsd, BPS, BPS + liquidationBonusBps);\n        return Math.mulDiv(valueUsd, loanScale, WAD);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Borrow totals as they would be after `accrueInterest()`, for view functions.\n    function _projectedBorrowTotals() private view returns (uint256 borrowAssets, uint256 borrowShares_) {\n        borrowShares_ = totalBorrowShares;\n        borrowAssets = totalBorrowAssets + _pendingInterest();\n    }\n\n    function _projectedSupplyTotals() private view returns (uint256 supplyAssets, uint256 borrowAssets) {\n        uint256 interest = _pendingInterest();\n        supplyAssets = totalSupplyAssets + interest;\n        borrowAssets = totalBorrowAssets + interest;\n    }\n\n    function _pendingInterest() private view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrual;\n        if (elapsed == 0 || totalBorrowAssets == 0 || ratePerYear == 0) return 0;\n        return Math.mulDiv(totalBorrowAssets, ratePerYear * elapsed, WAD * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":446,"oldLines":11,"newStart":446,"newLines":9,"lines":["         return totalSupplyAssets.zeroFloorSub(totalBorrowAssets);","     }"," ","-    /// @notice Value of `shares` supply shares in loan tokens, interest included.","+    /// @notice Value of a lender's supply shares in loan tokens, interest included.","     function supplyAssetsOf(address lender) external view returns (uint256) {","-        (uint256 supplyAssets, uint256 borrowAssets) = _projectedSupplyTotals();","-        borrowAssets; // silence unused","-        return supplyShares[lender].toAssetsDown(supplyAssets, totalSupplyShares);","+        return supplyShares[lender].toAssetsDown(totalSupplyAssets + _pendingInterest(), totalSupplyShares);","     }"," ","     /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"438f3f26-fd48-4d7a-a3b3-acacfba75153","uuid":"3bb28726-9bc0-4ddf-bd6c-73c23b6a21da","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:33:33.837Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122805Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/src/BorrowMarket.sol","oldString":"    function _projectedSupplyTotals() private view returns (uint256 supplyAssets, uint256 borrowAssets) {\n        uint256 interest = _pendingInterest();\n        supplyAssets = totalSupplyAssets + interest;\n        borrowAssets = totalBorrowAssets + interest;\n    }\n\n    function _pendingInterest()","newString":"    function _pendingInterest()","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport {Ownable2Step} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol\";\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {IERC20Metadata} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/IERC20Metadata.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport {Math} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol\";\nimport {Pausable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Pausable.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\n\nimport {IPriceOracle} from \"./interfaces/IPriceOracle.sol\";\nimport {SharesMathLib} from \"./libraries/SharesMathLib.sol\";\n\n/// @title BorrowMarket\n/// @notice Single-pair overcollateralised borrowing market: lock WETH, borrow USDC.\n///\n/// Lenders supply the loan token and receive shares in the pool; borrowers post collateral and draw\n/// loan tokens against it up to `ltvBps` of the collateral's value. Debt accrues at a flat annual rate.\n/// Once debt exceeds `liquidationThresholdBps` of collateral value, anyone may repay part of the debt\n/// and seize the matching collateral plus `liquidationBonusBps`.\n///\n/// @dev Accounting is share-based on both sides. Interest is materialised into `totalBorrowAssets` /\n///      `totalSupplyAssets` by `accrueInterest()`, which every state-changing entrypoint calls first.\n///      Balances are tracked internally and never read from `balanceOf`, so token donations to this\n///      contract cannot move the exchange rate.\ncontract BorrowMarket is Ownable2Step, Pausable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n    using SharesMathLib for uint256;\n\n    uint256 internal constant WAD = 1e18;\n    uint256 internal constant BPS = 1e4;\n    uint256 internal constant SECONDS_PER_YEAR = 365 days;\n\n    /// @notice Hard ceiling on the configurable borrow rate: 100% APR.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_RATE_PER_YEAR = 1e18;\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                 Immutables                                 */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Collateral asset (WETH on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable collateralToken;\n    /// @notice Borrowable asset (USDC on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable loanToken;\n\n    /// @dev 10 ** decimals of each token, captured once at deploy.\n    uint256 internal immutable collateralScale;\n    uint256 internal immutable loanScale;\n\n    /// @notice Max debt as a fraction of collateral value when *taking on* risk (borrow / withdraw collateral).\n    uint256 public immutable ltvBps;\n    /// @notice Debt/collateral ratio at which a position becomes liquidatable.\n    uint256 public immutable liquidationThresholdBps;\n    /// @notice Extra collateral a liquidator receives, on top of the value repaid.\n    uint256 public immutable liquidationBonusBps;\n    /// @notice Max fraction of a position's debt that a single liquidation may repay while the position\n    ///         is still solvent. Once collateral value no longer covers the debt, full repayment is allowed\n    ///         so the bad debt can be cleared.\n    uint256 public immutable closeFactorBps;\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                   Storage                                  */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    struct Position {\n        uint256 collateral;\n        uint256 borrowShares;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Price source for the collateral asset.\n    IPriceOracle public oracle;\n    /// @notice Flat annual interest rate, 1e18 = 100% APR.\n    uint256 public ratePerYear;\n\n    uint256 public totalSupplyAssets;\n    uint256 public totalSupplyShares;\n    uint256 public totalBorrowAssets;\n    uint256 public totalBorrowShares;\n    uint256 public lastAccrual;\n\n    mapping(address => uint256) public supplyShares;\n    mapping(address => Position) public positions;\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                   Events                                   */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    event Supply(address indexed caller, address indexed onBehalf, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event Withdraw(address indexed caller, address indexed receiver, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event DepositCollateral(address indexed caller, address indexed onBehalf, uint256 amount);\n    event WithdrawCollateral(address indexed caller, address indexed receiver, uint256 amount);\n    event Borrow(address indexed borrower, address indexed receiver, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event Repay(address indexed caller, address indexed onBehalf, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event Liquidate(\n        address indexed liquidator, address indexed borrower, uint256 repaidAssets, uint256 repaidShares, uint256 seized\n    );\n    event BadDebtRealised(address indexed borrower, uint256 assets, uint256 shares);\n    event AccrueInterest(uint256 interest, uint256 elapsed);\n    event OracleSet(address indexed oracle);\n    event RateSet(uint256 ratePerYear);\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                   Errors                                   */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error InvalidParameters();\n    error UnsupportedDecimals();\n    error RateTooHigh();\n    error InsufficientLiquidity();\n    error InsufficientShares();\n    error InsufficientCollateral();\n    error PositionUnhealthy();\n    error PositionHealthy();\n    error NoDebt();\n    error RepayExceedsCloseFactor();\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                 Constructor                                */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @param owner_ Admin address. Should be a timelocked multisig — see NOTES.md.\n    /// @param collateralToken_ WETH.\n    /// @param loanToken_ USDC.\n    /// @param oracle_ Collateral price source, 1e18-scaled USD per whole collateral unit.\n    /// @param ratePerYear_ Flat annual borrow rate, 1e18 = 100%.\n    /// @param ltvBps_ Borrow limit, e.g. 7000.\n    /// @param liquidationThresholdBps_ Liquidation trigger, e.g. 8500.\n    /// @param liquidationBonusBps_ Liquidator incentive, e.g. 500.\n    /// @param closeFactorBps_ Max share of debt repayable per liquidation while solvent, e.g. 5000.\n    constructor(\n        address owner_,\n        address collateralToken_,\n        address loanToken_,\n        address oracle_,\n        uint256 ratePerYear_,\n        uint256 ltvBps_,\n        uint256 liquidationThresholdBps_,\n        uint256 liquidationBonusBps_,\n        uint256 closeFactorBps_\n    ) Ownable(owner_) {\n        if (collateralToken_ == address(0) || loanToken_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (collateralToken_ == loanToken_) revert InvalidParameters();\n\n        // Ordering invariants. The last one is the important one: at the moment a position crosses the\n        // liquidation threshold, seizing collateral worth (1 + bonus) x the repaid debt must still be\n        // covered by the collateral on hand, otherwise every liquidation would immediately create bad debt.\n        if (ltvBps_ == 0 || ltvBps_ >= liquidationThresholdBps_) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (liquidationThresholdBps_ >= BPS) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (liquidationBonusBps_ == 0) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (closeFactorBps_ == 0 || closeFactorBps_ > BPS) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (liquidationThresholdBps_ * (BPS + liquidationBonusBps_) > BPS * BPS) revert InvalidParameters();\n        if (ratePerYear_ > MAX_RATE_PER_YEAR) revert RateTooHigh();\n\n        collateralToken = IERC20(collateralToken_);\n        loanToken = IERC20(loanToken_);\n\n        uint8 collateralDecimals = IERC20Metadata(collateralToken_).decimals();\n        uint8 loanDecimals = IERC20Metadata(loanToken_).decimals();\n        if (collateralDecimals > 18 || loanDecimals > 18) revert UnsupportedDecimals();\n        collateralScale = 10 ** collateralDecimals;\n        loanScale = 10 ** loanDecimals;\n\n        ltvBps = ltvBps_;\n        liquidationThresholdBps = liquidationThresholdBps_;\n        liquidationBonusBps = liquidationBonusBps_;\n        closeFactorBps = closeFactorBps_;\n\n        _setOracle(oracle_);\n        ratePerYear = ratePerYear_;\n        emit RateSet(ratePerYear_);\n\n        lastAccrual = block.timestamp;\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                  Interest                                  */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Materialise interest owed since the last touch. Safe to call by anyone at any time.\n    /// @dev Simple interest over the elapsed interval, which compounds each time the market is touched.\n    function accrueInterest() public {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrual;\n        if (elapsed == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 borrowed = totalBorrowAssets;\n        if (borrowed != 0 && ratePerYear != 0) {\n            uint256 interest = Math.mulDiv(borrowed, ratePerYear * elapsed, WAD * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n            if (interest != 0) {\n                totalBorrowAssets = borrowed + interest;\n                totalSupplyAssets += interest;\n                emit AccrueInterest(interest, elapsed);\n            }\n        }\n\n        lastAccrual = block.timestamp;\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                 Lender side                                */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Supply loan tokens to the pool and receive shares.\n    function supply(uint256 assets, address onBehalf) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused returns (uint256 shares) {\n        if (assets == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        shares = assets.toSharesDown(totalSupplyAssets, totalSupplyShares);\n        if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        supplyShares[onBehalf] += shares;\n        totalSupplyShares += shares;\n        totalSupplyAssets += assets;\n\n        emit Supply(msg.sender, onBehalf, assets, shares);\n        loanToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), assets);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Burn supply shares and withdraw the underlying loan tokens.\n    /// @dev Limited by idle liquidity: assets that are currently lent out cannot be withdrawn.\n    function withdraw(uint256 shares, address receiver) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 assets) {\n        if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (receiver == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        if (supplyShares[msg.sender] < shares) revert InsufficientShares();\n\n        assets = shares.toAssetsDown(totalSupplyAssets, totalSupplyShares);\n\n        supplyShares[msg.sender] -= shares;\n        totalSupplyShares -= shares;\n        totalSupplyAssets -= assets;\n\n        if (totalBorrowAssets > totalSupplyAssets) revert InsufficientLiquidity();\n\n        emit Withdraw(msg.sender, receiver, assets, shares);\n        loanToken.safeTransfer(receiver, assets);\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                Borrower side                               */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Add collateral to a position.\n    /// @dev Anyone may top up anyone's position; this can only improve the target's health.\n    function depositCollateral(uint256 amount, address onBehalf) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        positions[onBehalf].collateral += amount;\n\n        emit DepositCollateral(msg.sender, onBehalf, amount);\n        collateralToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Remove collateral from your own position. Must leave the position within the LTV limit.\n    function withdrawCollateral(uint256 amount, address receiver) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (receiver == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        Position storage position = positions[msg.sender];\n        if (position.collateral < amount) revert InsufficientCollateral();\n        position.collateral -= amount;\n\n        _requireWithinLtv(position);\n\n        emit WithdrawCollateral(msg.sender, receiver, amount);\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(receiver, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Borrow loan tokens against your own collateral.\n    function borrow(uint256 assets, address receiver) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused returns (uint256 shares) {\n        if (assets == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (receiver == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        shares = assets.toSharesUp(totalBorrowAssets, totalBorrowShares);\n\n        Position storage position = positions[msg.sender];\n        position.borrowShares += shares;\n        totalBorrowShares += shares;\n        totalBorrowAssets += assets;\n\n        if (totalBorrowAssets > totalSupplyAssets) revert InsufficientLiquidity();\n        _requireWithinLtv(position);\n\n        emit Borrow(msg.sender, receiver, assets, shares);\n        loanToken.safeTransfer(receiver, assets);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Repay debt on a position. Pass `type(uint256).max` to repay the position in full.\n    /// @dev Repaying is never paused and never health-checked — it can only make a position safer.\n    function repay(uint256 assets, address onBehalf) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 shares) {\n        if (onBehalf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        Position storage position = positions[onBehalf];\n        uint256 borrowShares_ = position.borrowShares;\n        if (borrowShares_ == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        if (assets == type(uint256).max) {\n            // Full exit: price the caller's whole share balance, rounding the amount owed up.\n            shares = borrowShares_;\n            assets = shares.toAssetsUp(totalBorrowAssets, totalBorrowShares);\n        } else {\n            if (assets == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n            shares = assets.toSharesDown(totalBorrowAssets, totalBorrowShares);\n            if (shares == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n            if (shares > borrowShares_) revert InsufficientShares();\n        }\n\n        position.borrowShares = borrowShares_ - shares;\n        totalBorrowShares -= shares;\n        totalBorrowAssets = totalBorrowAssets.zeroFloorSub(assets);\n\n        emit Repay(msg.sender, onBehalf, assets, shares);\n        loanToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), assets);\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                 Liquidation                                */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Repay part of an unhealthy position's debt and seize the matching collateral plus bonus.\n    /// @param borrower Position to liquidate.\n    /// @param repayAssets Loan-token amount to repay. Pass `type(uint256).max` to repay the maximum allowed.\n    /// @return repaid Loan tokens actually pulled from the liquidator.\n    /// @return seized Collateral tokens sent to the liquidator.\n    /// @dev `repaid` may come out below `repayAssets` when the position has less collateral than the\n    ///      requested repayment would seize; the seizure is clamped to the collateral on hand and the\n    ///      repayment recomputed from it. Liquidators should simulate first and set slippage bounds.\n    function liquidate(address borrower, uint256 repayAssets)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        whenNotPaused\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        accrueInterest();\n\n        Position storage position = positions[borrower];\n        uint256 borrowShares_ = position.borrowShares;\n        uint256 collateral = position.collateral;\n        if (borrowShares_ == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        uint256 price = oracle.price();\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueInLoanUnits(collateral, price);\n        uint256 debt = borrowShares_.toAssetsUp(totalBorrowAssets, totalBorrowShares);\n\n        // Healthy positions are off limits.\n        if (debt <= Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, liquidationThresholdBps, BPS)) revert PositionHealthy();\n\n        // While the position is still solvent the close factor bounds a single liquidation. Once the\n        // collateral no longer covers the debt, allow a full repayment so the position can be closed out.\n        uint256 maxRepay =\n            collateralValue <= debt ? debt : Math.mulDiv(debt, closeFactorBps, BPS, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        repaid = repayAssets == type(uint256).max ? maxRepay : repayAssets;\n        if (repaid == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (repaid > maxRepay) revert RepayExceedsCloseFactor();\n\n        seized = _seizeForRepay(repaid, price);\n        if (seized > collateral) {\n            seized = collateral;\n            repaid = _repayForSeize(seized, price);\n        }\n        if (seized == 0 || repaid == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        uint256 repaidShares = repaid.toSharesDown(totalBorrowAssets, totalBorrowShares);\n        if (repaidShares > borrowShares_) repaidShares = borrowShares_;\n\n        borrowShares_ -= repaidShares;\n        position.borrowShares = borrowShares_;\n        position.collateral = collateral - seized;\n        totalBorrowShares -= repaidShares;\n        totalBorrowAssets = totalBorrowAssets.zeroFloorSub(repaid);\n\n        emit Liquidate(msg.sender, borrower, repaid, repaidShares, seized);\n\n        // Collateral exhausted but debt remains: it is unrecoverable. Write it off now so that\n        // totalSupplyAssets stops counting money that will never come back, and lenders share the loss\n        // pro rata at today's exchange rate instead of the last one out taking it all.\n        if (position.collateral == 0 && borrowShares_ != 0) {\n            uint256 badDebt = borrowShares_.toAssetsUp(totalBorrowAssets, totalBorrowShares);\n\n            position.borrowShares = 0;\n            totalBorrowShares -= borrowShares_;\n            totalBorrowAssets = totalBorrowAssets.zeroFloorSub(badDebt);\n            totalSupplyAssets = totalSupplyAssets.zeroFloorSub(badDebt);\n\n            emit BadDebtRealised(borrower, badDebt, borrowShares_);\n        }\n\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(msg.sender, seized);\n        loanToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), repaid);\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                    Views                                   */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Debt of a position including interest accrued up to now, rounded up.\n    function debtOf(address borrower) public view returns (uint256) {\n        (uint256 borrowAssets, uint256 borrowShares_) = _projectedBorrowTotals();\n        return positions[borrower].borrowShares.toAssetsUp(borrowAssets, borrowShares_);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Collateral of a position valued in loan-token units at the current oracle price.\n    function collateralValueOf(address borrower) public view returns (uint256) {\n        return _collateralValueInLoanUnits(positions[borrower].collateral, oracle.price());\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Loan tokens a position could still borrow before hitting the LTV limit.\n    function maxBorrow(address borrower) external view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 limit = Math.mulDiv(collateralValueOf(borrower), ltvBps, BPS);\n        uint256 debt = debtOf(borrower);\n        uint256 headroom = limit.zeroFloorSub(debt);\n        uint256 liquidity = totalSupplyAssets.zeroFloorSub(totalBorrowAssets);\n        return Math.min(headroom, liquidity);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice False once debt exceeds `liquidationThresholdBps` of collateral value, i.e. liquidatable.\n    function isHealthy(address borrower) external view returns (bool) {\n        return debtOf(borrower) <= Math.mulDiv(collateralValueOf(borrower), liquidationThresholdBps, BPS);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Collateral value over debt, 1e18-scaled; below 1e18 the position is liquidatable.\n    /// @dev `type(uint256).max` for a position with no debt.\n    function healthFactor(address borrower) external view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 debt = debtOf(borrower);\n        if (debt == 0) return type(uint256).max;\n        uint256 limit = Math.mulDiv(collateralValueOf(borrower), liquidationThresholdBps, BPS);\n        return Math.mulDiv(limit, WAD, debt);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Loan tokens available to borrow or withdraw right now.\n    function availableLiquidity() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return totalSupplyAssets.zeroFloorSub(totalBorrowAssets);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Value of a lender's supply shares in loan tokens, interest included.\n    function supplyAssetsOf(address lender) external view returns (uint256) {\n        return supplyShares[lender].toAssetsDown(totalSupplyAssets + _pendingInterest(), totalSupplyShares);\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                    Admin                                   */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Point the market at a new price source.\n    /// @dev Highest-impact admin power in the system: a bad oracle can make every position liquidatable.\n    ///      Owner must be a timelock. See NOTES.md.\n    function setOracle(address oracle_) external onlyOwner {\n        _setOracle(oracle_);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Change the flat borrow rate. Interest up to this point is settled at the old rate first.\n    function setRatePerYear(uint256 ratePerYear_) external onlyOwner {\n        if (ratePerYear_ > MAX_RATE_PER_YEAR) revert RateTooHigh();\n        accrueInterest();\n        ratePerYear = ratePerYear_;\n        emit RateSet(ratePerYear_);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Halt supply, borrow, collateral moves and liquidations. Repayment stays open.\n    function pause() external onlyOwner {\n        accrueInterest();\n        _pause();\n    }\n\n    function unpause() external onlyOwner {\n        // Bring `lastAccrual` to now before reopening so the paused interval is not charged as interest\n        // at whatever the rate happens to be on unpause.\n        accrueInterest();\n        _unpause();\n    }\n\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                  Internals                                 */\n    /* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    function _setOracle(address oracle_) private {\n        if (oracle_ == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        // Fail fast on a feed that is already broken rather than discovering it on the next borrow.\n        if (IPriceOracle(oracle_).price() == 0) revert InvalidParameters();\n        oracle = IPriceOracle(oracle_);\n        emit OracleSet(oracle_);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Reverts unless the position's debt is within `ltvBps` of its collateral value.\n    ///      Callers must have accrued interest first.\n    function _requireWithinLtv(Position storage position) private view {\n        uint256 debt = position.borrowShares.toAssetsUp(totalBorrowAssets, totalBorrowShares);\n        if (debt == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueInLoanUnits(position.collateral, oracle.price());\n        if (debt > Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, ltvBps, BPS)) revert PositionUnhealthy();\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Collateral amount -> loan-token units at `price` (1e18 USD per whole collateral unit).\n    ///      Rounds down, so the protocol never overstates what a position is worth.\n    function _collateralValueInLoanUnits(uint256 collateral, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 valueUsd = Math.mulDiv(collateral, price, collateralScale);\n        return Math.mulDiv(valueUsd, loanScale, WAD);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Collateral owed to a liquidator for repaying `repayAssets`, including the bonus. Rounds down.\n    function _seizeForRepay(uint256 repayAssets, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 valueUsd = Math.mulDiv(repayAssets, WAD, loanScale);\n        uint256 withBonus = Math.mulDiv(valueUsd, BPS + liquidationBonusBps, BPS);\n        return Math.mulDiv(withBonus, collateralScale, price);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Inverse of `_seizeForRepay`, used when the seizure is clamped to the collateral on hand.\n    ///      Rounds down so the clamped seizure is never under-collateralised relative to the repayment.\n    function _repayForSeize(uint256 seizeAmount, uint256 price) private view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 withBonusUsd = Math.mulDiv(seizeAmount, price, collateralScale);\n        uint256 valueUsd = Math.mulDiv(withBonusUsd, BPS, BPS + liquidationBonusBps);\n        return Math.mulDiv(valueUsd, loanScale, WAD);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Borrow totals as they would be after `accrueInterest()`, for view functions.\n    function _projectedBorrowTotals() private view returns (uint256 borrowAssets, uint256 borrowShares_) {\n        borrowShares_ = totalBorrowShares;\n        borrowAssets = totalBorrowAssets + _pendingInterest();\n    }\n\n    function _projectedSupplyTotals() private view returns (uint256 supplyAssets, uint256 borrowAssets) {\n        uint256 interest = _pendingInterest();\n        supplyAssets = totalSupplyAssets + interest;\n        borrowAssets = totalBorrowAssets + interest;\n    }\n\n    function _pendingInterest() private view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrual;\n        if (elapsed == 0 || totalBorrowAssets == 0 || ratePerYear == 0) return 0;\n        return Math.mulDiv(totalBorrowAssets, ratePerYear * elapsed, WAD * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":533,"oldLines":12,"newStart":533,"newLines":6,"lines":["         borrowAssets = totalBorrowAssets + _pendingInterest();","     }"," ","-    function _projectedSupplyTotals() private view returns (uint256 supplyAssets, uint256 borrowAssets) {","-        uint256 interest = _pendingInterest();","-        supplyAssets = totalSupplyAssets + interest;","-        borrowAssets = totalBorrowAssets + interest;","-    }","-","     function _pendingInterest() private view returns (uint256) {","         uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrual;","         if (elapsed == 0 || totalBorrowAssets == 0 || ratePerYear == 0) return 0;"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUTmbrXNXBN732yWNZym","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_015KaAHdwr4voHdA4VcWEMRD","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122805Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/script/Deploy.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Script} from \"forge-std/Script.sol\";\nimport {console2} from \"forge-std/console2.sol\";\n\nimport {BorrowMarket} from \"../src/BorrowMarket.sol\";\nimport {ChainlinkPriceOracle} from \"../src/ChainlinkPriceOracle.sol\";\n\n/// @notice Deploys the WETH/USDC market to Ethereum mainnet.\n/// @dev Run with `forge script script/Deploy.s.sol --rpc-url $ETH_RPC_URL --broadcast`.\n///      `OWNER` must be set to a timelocked multisig — see NOTES.md.\ncontract Deploy is Script {\n    // Ethereum mainnet.\n    address constant WETH = 0xC02aaA39b223FE8D0A0e5C4F27eAD9083C756Cc2;\n    address constant USDC = 0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48;\n    // Chainlink ETH/USD, 8 decimals, 0.5% deviation / 1h heartbeat.\n    address constant ETH_USD_FEED = 0x5f4eC3Df9cbd43714FE2740f5E3616155c5b8419;\n\n    // Feed heartbeat is 3600s; allow one missed beat plus slack before the market freezes.\n    uint256 constant MAX_PRICE_AGE = 4500;\n    // Sanity band on ETH/USD, 1e18-scaled. Wide enough to never trip on normal moves.\n    uint256 constant MIN_ETH_PRICE = 100e18;\n    uint256 constant MAX_ETH_PRICE = 100_000e18;\n\n    uint256 constant RATE_PER_YEAR = 0.05e18; // 5% APR, flat\n    uint256 constant LTV_BPS = 7000;\n    uint256 constant LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS = 8500;\n    uint256 constant LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS = 500;\n    uint256 constant CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS = 5000;\n\n    function run() external returns (ChainlinkPriceOracle oracle, BorrowMarket market) {\n        address owner = vm.envAddress(\"OWNER\");\n\n        vm.startBroadcast();\n\n        oracle = new ChainlinkPriceOracle(owner, ETH_USD_FEED, MAX_PRICE_AGE, MIN_ETH_PRICE, MAX_ETH_PRICE);\n\n        market = new BorrowMarket(\n            owner,\n            WETH,\n            USDC,\n            address(oracle),\n            RATE_PER_YEAR,\n            LTV_BPS,\n            LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS,\n            LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS,\n            CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS\n        );\n\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n\n        console2.log(\"oracle\", address(oracle));\n        console2.log(\"market\", address(market));\n        console2.log(\"owner \", owner);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":565,"cache_read_input_tokens":51556,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":565},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"438f3f26-fd48-4d7a-a3b3-acacfba75153","uuid":"5639cd33-bb92-45d2-b1ea-aa76817d65d6","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:33:47.639Z","request_id":"req_011CdUTmaNEFTqWQpH7vbFbP"}

Wide enough to never trip on normal moves.\n    uint256 constant MIN_ETH_PRICE = 100e18;\n    uint256 constant MAX_ETH_PRICE = 100_000e18;\n\n    uint256 constant RATE_PER_YEAR = 0.05e18; // 5% APR, flat\n    uint256 constant LTV_BPS = 7000;\n    uint256 constant LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS = 8500;\n    uint256 constant LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS = 500;\n    uint256 constant CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS = 5000;\n\n    function run() external returns (ChainlinkPriceOracle oracle, BorrowMarket market) {\n        address owner = vm.envAddress(\"OWNER\");\n\n        vm.startBroadcast();\n\n        oracle = new ChainlinkPriceOracle(owner, ETH_USD_FEED, MAX_PRICE_AGE, MIN_ETH_PRICE, MAX_ETH_PRICE);\n\n        market = new BorrowMarket(\n            owner,\n            WETH,\n            USDC,\n            address(oracle),\n            RATE_PER_YEAR,\n            LTV_BPS,\n            LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS,\n            LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS,\n            CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS\n        );\n\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n\n        console2.log(\"oracle\", address(oracle));\n        console2.log(\"market\", address(market));\n        console2.log(\"owner \", owner);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"438f3f26-fd48-4d7a-a3b3-acacfba75153","uuid":"96a102ec-56e2-4e7c-a627-9761a7d70ed9","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:34:03.734Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122805Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/src/ChainlinkPriceOracle.sol","oldString":"        uint256 normalised = uint256(answer) * scaleUp;","newString":"        // casting to 'uint256' is safe because `answer > 0` is checked above\n        // forge-lint: disable-next-line(unsafe-typecast)\n        uint256 normalised = uint256(answer) * scaleUp;","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport {Ownable2Step} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol\";\n\nimport {AggregatorV3Interface} from \"./interfaces/AggregatorV3Interface.sol\";\nimport {IPriceOracle} from \"./interfaces/IPriceOracle.sol\";\n\n/// @title ChainlinkPriceOracle\n/// @notice Wraps a Chainlink USD feed (e.g. mainnet ETH/USD) and normalises it to 1e18.\n/// @dev Every read is validated. A bad or stale feed makes this revert, which freezes borrowing,\n///      collateral withdrawals and liquidations rather than letting them run on a wrong number.\ncontract ChainlinkPriceOracle is IPriceOracle, Ownable2Step {\n    uint256 private constant WAD = 1e18;\n\n    /// @notice Underlying Chainlink aggregator proxy.\n    AggregatorV3Interface public immutable feed;\n\n    /// @dev 10 ** (18 - feed.decimals()), applied to normalise the answer to 1e18.\n    uint256 private immutable scaleUp;\n\n    /// @notice Maximum age of a round before the price is considered stale (seconds).\n    uint256 public maxAge;\n\n    /// @notice Inclusive sanity band for the normalised price, in 1e18 USD.\n    /// @dev Guards against an aggregator pinned at its own min/max answer during a market dislocation\n    ///      or a misconfigured feed. Set wide enough that normal volatility never trips it.\n    uint256 public minPrice;\n    uint256 public maxPrice;\n\n    event MaxAgeSet(uint256 maxAge);\n    event PriceBoundsSet(uint256 minPrice, uint256 maxPrice);\n\n    error UnsupportedFeedDecimals(uint8 decimals);\n    error InvalidBounds();\n    error InvalidMaxAge();\n    error NonPositiveAnswer(int256 answer);\n    error IncompleteRound();\n    error FutureTimestamp(uint256 updatedAt);\n    error StalePrice(uint256 updatedAt, uint256 maxAge);\n    error PriceOutOfBounds(uint256 price);\n\n    constructor(address owner_, address feed_, uint256 maxAge_, uint256 minPrice_, uint256 maxPrice_) Ownable(owner_) {\n        feed = AggregatorV3Interface(feed_);\n\n        uint8 feedDecimals = feed.decimals();\n        if (feedDecimals > 18) revert UnsupportedFeedDecimals(feedDecimals);\n        scaleUp = 10 ** (18 - feedDecimals);\n\n        _setMaxAge(maxAge_);\n        _setPriceBounds(minPrice_, maxPrice_);\n    }\n\n    /// @inheritdoc IPriceOracle\n    function price() external view returns (uint256) {\n        (uint80 roundId, int256 answer,, uint256 updatedAt, uint80 answeredInRound) = feed.latestRoundData();\n\n        if (answer <= 0) revert NonPositiveAnswer(answer);\n        if (updatedAt == 0 || answeredInRound < roundId) revert IncompleteRound();\n        if (updatedAt > block.timestamp) revert FutureTimestamp(updatedAt);\n        if (block.timestamp - updatedAt > maxAge) revert StalePrice(updatedAt, maxAge);\n\n        uint256 normalised = uint256(answer) * scaleUp;\n        if (normalised < minPrice || normalised > maxPrice) revert PriceOutOfBounds(normalised);\n\n        return normalised;\n    }\n\n    function setMaxAge(uint256 maxAge_) external onlyOwner {\n        _setMaxAge(maxAge_);\n    }\n\n    function setPriceBounds(uint256 minPrice_, uint256 maxPrice_) external onlyOwner {\n        _setPriceBounds(minPrice_, maxPrice_);\n    }\n\n    function _setMaxAge(uint256 maxAge_) private {\n        if (maxAge_ == 0) revert InvalidMaxAge();\n        maxAge = maxAge_;\n        emit MaxAgeSet(maxAge_);\n    }\n\n    function _setPriceBounds(uint256 minPrice_, uint256 maxPrice_) private {\n        if (minPrice_ == 0 || minPrice_ >= maxPrice_) revert InvalidBounds();\n        minPrice = minPrice_;\n        maxPrice = maxPrice_;\n        emit PriceBoundsSet(minPrice_, maxPrice_);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":61,"oldLines":6,"newStart":61,"newLines":8,"lines":["         if (updatedAt > block.timestamp) revert FutureTimestamp(updatedAt);","         if (block.timestamp - updatedAt > maxAge) revert StalePrice(updatedAt, maxAge);"," ","+        // casting to 'uint256' is safe because `answer > 0` is checked above","+        // forge-lint: disable-next-line(unsafe-typecast)","         uint256 normalised = uint256(answer) * scaleUp;","         if (normalised < minPrice || normalised > maxPrice) revert PriceOutOfBounds(normalised);"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUTpXp2UBD8Mb8NjgH8y","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XxtaJAfHFp497o8YUpXsS9","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122805Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/test/BorrowMarket.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\n\nimport {BorrowMarket} from \"../src/BorrowMarket.sol\";\nimport {MockERC20, MockOracle} from \"./mocks/Mocks.sol\";\n\ncontract BorrowMarketTest is Test {\n    MockERC20 weth;\n    MockERC20 usdc;\n    MockOracle oracle;\n    BorrowMarket market;\n\n    address owner = makeAddr(\"owner\");\n    address lender = makeAddr(\"lender\");\n    address borrower = makeAddr(\"borrower\");\n    address liquidator = makeAddr(\"liquidator\");\n\n    uint256 constant ETH_PRICE = 2000e18; // $2000 / ETH\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        weth = new MockERC20(\"Wrapped Ether\", \"WETH\", 18);\n        usdc = new MockERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\", 6);\n        oracle = new MockOracle(ETH_PRICE);\n\n        market = new BorrowMarket(owner, address(weth), address(usdc), address(oracle), 0.05e18, 7000, 8500, 500, 5000);\n\n        usdc.mint(lender, 1_000_000e6);\n        usdc.mint(liquidator, 1_000_000e6);\n        weth.mint(borrower, 100e18);\n\n        vm.prank(lender);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.prank(borrower);\n        weth.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.startPrank(borrower);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n        vm.prank(liquidator);\n        usdc.approve(address(market), type(uint256).max);\n\n        vm.prank(lender);\n        market.supply(500_000e6, lender);\n    }\n\n    function _openPosition(uint256 collateral, uint256 debt) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(borrower);\n        market.depositCollateral(collateral, borrower);\n        market.borrow(debt, borrower);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_borrowUpToLtvAndNoFurther() public {\n        // 10 WETH @ $2000 = $20,000 collateral -> 70% LTV = 14,000 USDC\n        vm.startPrank(borrower);\n        market.depositCollateral(10e18, borrower);\n        assertEq(market.maxBorrow(borrower), 14_000e6);\n        market.borrow(14_000e6, borrower);\n\n        vm.expectRevert(BorrowMarket.PositionUnhealthy.selector);\n        market.borrow(1e6, borrower);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(borrower), 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(market.debtOf(borrower), 14_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_interestAccrues() public {\n        _openPosition(10e18, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n\n        // 5% flat on 10,000 for one year.\n        assertApproxEqAbs(market.debtOf(borrower), 10_500e6, 1);\n        assertApproxEqAbs(market.supplyAssetsOf(lender), 500_500e6, 1);\n    }\n\n    function test_repayAndWithdrawCollateral() public {\n        _openPosition(10e18, 10_000e6);\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 30 days);\n\n        uint256 owed = market.debtOf(borrower);\n        usdc.mint(borrower, owed); // cover accrued interest\n\n        vm.startPrank(borrower);\n        market.repay(type(uint256).max, borrower);\n        assertEq(market.debtOf(borrower), 0);\n\n        market.withdrawCollateral(10e18, borrower);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(borrower), 100e18);\n    }\n\n    function test_withdrawCollateralBlockedWhenItBreaksLtv() public {\n        _openPosition(10e18, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(borrower);\n        vm.expectRevert(BorrowMarket.PositionUnhealthy.selector);\n        market.withdrawCollateral(1e18, borrower);\n    }\n\n    function test_healthyPositionCannotBeLiquidated() public {\n        _openPosition(10e18, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(liquidator);\n        vm.expectRevert(BorrowMarket.PositionHealthy.selector);\n        market.liquidate(borrower, 1000e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_liquidationSeizesRepayPlusBonus() public {\n        _openPosition(10e18, 14_000e6);\n        assertTrue(market.isHealthy(borrower));\n\n        // Drop ETH to $1600: collateral = $16,000, debt/collateral = 87.5% > 85%.\n        oracle.set(1600e18);\n        assertFalse(market.isHealthy(borrower));\n\n        uint256 repay = 5_000e6; // within the 50% close factor\n        uint256 wethBefore = weth.balanceOf(liquidator);\n\n        vm.prank(liquidator);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = market.liquidate(borrower, repay);\n\n        assertEq(repaid, repay);\n        // $5,000 * 1.05 / $1600 = 3.28125 WETH\n        assertEq(seized, 3.28125e18);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(liquidator) - wethBefore, seized);\n        assertEq(market.debtOf(borrower), 9_000e6);\n        assertTrue(market.isHealthy(borrower));\n    }\n\n    function test_liquidationRespectsCloseFactorWhileSolvent() public {\n        _openPosition(10e18, 14_000e6);\n        oracle.set(1600e18);\n\n        vm.prank(liquidator);\n        vm.expectRevert(BorrowMarket.RepayExceedsCloseFactor.selector);\n        market.liquidate(borrower, 7_001e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_insolventPositionIsFullyClosedAndBadDebtSocialised() public {\n        _openPosition(10e18, 14_000e6);\n\n        // Crash below the debt: collateral = $13,000 < $14,000 debt.\n        oracle.set(1300e18);\n\n        uint256 supplyBefore = market.totalSupplyAssets();\n\n        vm.prank(liquidator);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = market.liquidate(borrower, type(uint256).max);\n\n        // Seizure clamped to the 10 WETH on hand; repayment recomputed from it.\n        assertEq(seized, 10e18);\n        assertEq(repaid, 12_380_952_380); // 10 * 1300 / 1.05, floored, in 6dp\n\n        (uint256 collateral, uint256 borrowShares) = market.positions(borrower);\n        assertEq(collateral, 0);\n        assertEq(borrowShares, 0, \"residual debt must be written off\");\n        assertEq(market.totalBorrowAssets(), 0);\n\n        // Lenders absorbed the shortfall.\n        assertLt(market.totalSupplyAssets(), supplyBefore);\n        assertApproxEqAbs(supplyBefore - market.totalSupplyAssets(), 14_000e6 - repaid, 2);\n    }\n\n    function test_lenderWithdrawLimitedByIdleLiquidity() public {\n        _openPosition(200e18, 400_000e6);\n\n        uint256 shares = market.supplyShares(lender);\n        vm.prank(lender);\n        vm.expectRevert(BorrowMarket.InsufficientLiquidity.selector);\n        market.withdraw(shares, lender);\n    }\n\n    function test_donationDoesNotMoveExchangeRate() public {\n        uint256 before = market.supplyAssetsOf(lender);\n        usdc.mint(address(market), 250_000e6);\n        assertEq(market.supplyAssetsOf(lender), before);\n    }\n\n    function test_repayIsAllowedWhilePaused() public {\n        _openPosition(10e18, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        market.pause();\n\n        vm.prank(borrower);\n        market.repay(1_000e6, borrower);\n        assertEq(market.debtOf(borrower), 9_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(borrower);\n        vm.expectRevert();\n        market.borrow(1e6, borrower);\n    }\n\n    function test_constructorRejectsBonusThatBreaksThreshold() public {\n        // 8500 * 1.20 > 100% -> every liquidation at the threshold would create bad debt.\n        vm.expectRevert(BorrowMarket.InvalidParameters.selector);\n        new BorrowMarket(owner, address(weth), address(usdc), address(oracle), 0.05e18, 7000, 8500, 2000, 5000);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":849,"cache_read_input_tokens":55639,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":849},"output_tokens":62,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"438f3f26-fd48-4d7a-a3b3-acacfba75153","uuid":"ae26ba56-e9a5-4882-ae78-d4448d257f06","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:34:44.411Z","request_id":"req_011CdUTpWNhoKMUrMfBBhNhq"}
